TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型在3D CT影像报告自动生成中的视觉令牌预算分配问题，横跨四种视觉编码器、五种令牌压缩投影器和五种指令微调LLM，在CT-RATE与Merlin两个大规模CT报告数据集上验证。结果表明解剖引导的感兴趣区域裁剪是最一致有效的策略（平均提升+3.7点），而更高分辨率输入的效果强烈依赖于投影器类型。最优配置在CT-RATE上达到49.5、Merlin上达到49.0的临床宏F1分数，取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT全卷影像在视觉语言模型中的应用面临严重计算瓶颈：现代视觉编码器每扫描可产生数万视觉令牌，成为LLM的主要计算负担。虽然视觉-语言投影器可压缩令牌序列降低计算，但过度压缩可能丢失临床关键细节；有效压缩则可在固定下游令牌数下支持更高分辨率输入。因此，如何在不同输入视野、空间分辨率和投影方式之间分配视觉令牌预算，是一个尚未解决的关键设计问题。

Method: 系统化消融评估框架：1) 四种异质视觉编码器（CNN和ViT两大类，包括3D ViT Primus和基于切片的2D ViT Curia等）；2) 五种令牌压缩投影器（最高64倍压缩）外加不压缩的MLP投影器基线；3) 五种指令微调LLM（参数量1.7B-4B）；4) 两个大规模CT报告数据集（CT-RATE和Merlin）。在匹配LLM令牌预算的条件下，对比不同视野选择（解剖引导ROI裁剪vs全卷）、输入分辨率以及投影策略的组合效果。

Result: 解剖引导的ROI裁剪在20种设置中的19种取得临床宏F1提升：对3D ViT Primus编码器平均+3.7点，对切片式2D ViT Curia编码器平均+1.1点。增加输入分辨率的效果强烈依赖投影器选择：PerceiverResampler配合更高分辨率的Curia特征在两个数据集上均取得最强结果。最佳配置在CT-RATE上达到49.5、Merlin上达到49.0的临床宏F1，均为SOTA。

Conclusion: 视觉令牌预算应优先分配给解剖引导的关键区域而非均匀覆盖全卷；投影器的选择直接决定了能否有效利用更高分辨率的输入。该研究为3D医学影像中视觉语言模型的设计提供了系统性的设计准则，并强调解剖先验与投影策略的协同优化是提升CT报告生成临床性能的关键。

Abstract: Vision-language models offer a promising path toward automating radiology report generation, but applying them to full 3D CT volumes poses substantial computational challenges. Modern foundation vision encoders (VEs) can produce tens of thousands of vision tokens per scan, making the visual sequence passed to the large language model (LLM) a primary computational bottleneck. Vision-to-language projectors can compress this sequence to reduce computation, but may discard clinically relevant detail; conversely, effective compression can accommodate higher-resolution inputs while keeping the downstream token count fixed. How this vision-token budget should be allocated across input field of view, spatial resolution, and vision-to-language projection therefore remains an open design question. We systematically evaluate four heterogeneous VEs (CNN- and ViT-based), five token-reducing projectors at up to 64x compression alongside a non-reducing MLP projector baseline, and five instruction-tuned LLMs (1.7B--4B) on two large-scale CT report datasets (CT-RATE and Merlin). At matched LLM token budgets, anatomy-guided region of interest cropping is the most consistent strategy, improving clinical macro F1 in 19 of 20 settings by +3.7 points on average for the 3D ViT Primus encoder and +1.1 for the slice-based 2D ViT Curia encoder. Increasing input resolution further is strongly projector-dependent: the PerceiverResampler, paired with higher-resolution Curia features, yields the strongest configuration in the resolution study on both datasets. Our best configurations achieve state-of-the-art clinical macro F1 on the test sets, reaching 49.5 on CT-RATE and 49.0 on Merlin. Code and models will be published upon publication.

</details>


### [133] [High-Quality Exposure Correction with Diffusion-Based Image Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08720)
*Ziwen Li,Meng Cao,Jinpu Zhang,Chunyang Li,Long Bao,Heng Sun,Yuehuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPEC（基于扩散先验的曝光校正）框架，利用预训练大规模扩散模型中的图像生成先验，通过高效微调策略实现单步去噪的曝光校正，并结合扩散模型与回归模型设计联合交叉注意力模块整合多尺度扩散先验特征，在多个曝光校正数据集上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曝光校正方法过度追求逐像素的全局精度，难以有效建模极端曝光区域，导致感知质量不佳。虽然扩散模型在图像生成领域表现卓越，但如何将其成功应用于曝光校正仍是一个开放且具有挑战性的问题，关键在于随机扩散过程中既要生成准确图像结构又要保持高保真度。

Method: 1) 提出高效微调策略，从预训练大规模扩散模型中导出曝光校正器，实现单步去噪生成增强图像；2) 融合扩散模型与回归模型的优势，设计联合交叉注意力模块整合多尺度扩散先验特征，有效保留高频细节并抑制随机伪影；3) 将扩散模型的职责限定为处理低频内容，而非所有复杂的纹理细节。

Result: 在多个曝光校正数据集上，DPEC方法在保真度、感知质量和视觉效果方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DPEC通过有效利用扩散先验、合理划分高低频处理职责以及融合回归模型策略，成功解决了扩散模型应用于曝光校正时保真度与结构准确性不足的问题，证明了扩散先验在底层视觉任务中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Although most existing exposure correction methods achieve high fidelity, they often place excessive focus on overall pixel-wise accuracy, making it challenging to effectively model extreme exposure regions, which results in suboptimal perceptual quality. Recently, diffusion models have received significant attention due to their remarkable performance in the realm of image generation. However, their successful application to exposure correction remains a challenging and open question. The key challenge lies in generating accurate image structures and maintaining high image fidelity during stochastic diffusion processes. In this paper, we propose DPEC (Diffusion Prior-based Exposure Correction), a novel framework for image exposure correction that utilizes diffusion-based image generation priors encapsulated in pre-trained large-scale diffusion models. Specifically, we first propose an efficient fine-tuning strategy to derive an exposure corrector from pre-trained models, enabling the generation of enhanced images in a single-step denoising process. Moreover, we seamlessly combine the strengths of diffusion models and regression models, and design a joint cross-attention module to integrate multi-scale diffusion prior features, thereby effectively preserving high-frequency details and minimizing random artifacts. The diffusion model focuses on dealing with low-frequency content rather than all the intricate texture details. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed DPEC method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods on multiple exposure correction datasets, whether in terms of fidelity, perceptual quality, or visual effects.

</details>


### [134] [TomaMMU: A Comprehensive Multimodal Understanding Benchmark for Tomato Leaf Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08727)
*Gia-Han Truong,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TomaMMU，一个大规模番茄叶病多模态理解数据集，包含28,808张图片、15个类别和213,119个人工标注的视觉问答对；同时提出TomaBench基准，将7项农业任务组织为三个层级（基础感知、病理理解、专家诊断）的层次化分类体系。对14个SOTA视觉语言模型（VLMs）的评估揭示了其在细粒度识别和事实性推理上的明显不足，而简单的微调可将挑战性多选题准确率提升至96.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型（VLMs）在植物病理学领域（尤其是番茄叶病）的细粒度识别和诊断推理能力存在显著不足，且缺乏大规模、高质量的领域专用多模态数据集来支撑系统性评估与训练。

Method: 构建TomaMMU数据集，采用三阶段流水线（数据采集、人工标注、问答生成）产出28,808张图像和213,119个VQA对；设计TomaBench基准，将7项农业任务按三级层次（基础感知、病理理解、专家诊断）组织，从低层视觉识别到高层诊断推理进行系统评估。

Result: 对14个SOTA VLMs的评估显示其在细粒度识别和事实性推理方面表现欠佳；在TomaMMU上简单微调后，挑战性MCQ准确率提升至96.09%，优于近期VLMs。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs难以将视觉感知有效转化为可靠的诊断知识，需要针对性的领域自适应；TomaMMU数据集与TomaBench基准为植物病理领域VLM的评估和改进提供了有效基础，微调展示了显著性能提升的潜力。

Abstract: To address this gap, we introduce TomaMMU, a large-scale Tomato leaf disease MultiModal Understanding dataset, alongside TomaBench, a benchmark for evaluating VLMs on tomato disease understanding. TomaMMU comprises 28,808 high-quality images spanning 15 categories and 213,119 human-annotated visual question-answer pairs, generated through a three-stage pipeline comprising Data Collection, Human Annotation, and Question-Answer Generation. Building on this foundation, TomaBench organizes seven agricultural tasks into a hierarchical three-level taxonomy spanning Basic Perception, Pathology Understanding, and Expert Diagnosis, which together enable systematic evaluation from low-level visual recognition to high-level diagnostic reasoning. The tasks assess visual symptom recognition, taxonomic relationships, and diagnostic reasoning, offering a comprehensive view of how well models grasp plant pathology. Our results pronounced gaps in fine-grained recognition and factually grounded reasoning with 14 state-of-the-art VLMs, consistently underperforming on both challenging MCQs and open-ended questions. These results suggest that current VLMs struggle to translate visual perception into reliable diagnostic knowledge, motivating the need for targeted domain adaptation. Simple fine-tuning on TomaMMU substantially narrows this gap, boosting accuracy on challenging MCQs to 96.09%, outperforming recent VLMs, and pointing toward promising directions for future work. All data and code is available in https://huggingface.co/datasets/enalis/TomaMMU.

</details>


### [135] [IDATA: Scalable Invertible Diffusion for Unrestricted Adversarial Transfer Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08734)
*Yi Pan,Jun-Jie Huang,Tianrui Liu,Zihan Chen,Lin Liu,Zhao Wentao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IDATA，一种面向无限制对抗迁移攻击的显存高效扩散框架，通过可逆扩散模块（IDM）实现常数显存的反向传播，并利用离散小波变换的低频约束模块（LFCM）将扰动限制在语义稳定的低频子空间，在攻击成功率、显存效率和视觉不可感知性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的攻击方法面临两大挑战：(1) 多步反向传播导致显存开销巨大；(2) 中间潜变量上的扰动缺乏频率感知，影响迁移性和不可感知性。这些问题限制了扩散攻击在黑盒鲁棒性评估中的实际应用。

Method: IDATA由两个核心组件构成：一是可逆扩散模块（IDM），将扩散轨迹上的对抗优化重构为可逆过程，通过按需重建中间状态而非存储完整去噪链，实现常数显存的反向传播；二是低频约束模块（LFCM），利用离散小波变换（DWT）将潜变量分解为低频和高频分量，将扰动限制在语义稳定的低频子空间，以提升迁移性并保持视觉不可感知性。

Result: 在多个基准测试和多种模型架构上的大量实验表明，IDATA在攻击成功率、显存效率和视觉不可感知性三个方面均持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: IDATA为深度视觉模型的黑盒鲁棒性评估提供了一种有前景的工具，兼顾了攻击性能与计算资源效率。

Abstract: Unrestricted adversarial transfer attacks are important for evaluating the black-box robustness of deep visual models. Diffusion-based attacks have shown promising transferability and visual imperceptibility by optimizing adversarial perturbations along denoising trajectories in latent space. However, existing methods are limited by two challenges: memory-intensive multistep backpropagation and frequency-agnostic perturbation over intermediate latents. To address these issues, we propose IDATA, a memory-efficient diffusion framework for unrestricted adversarial transfer attack. IDATA consists of two key components: an Invertible Diffusion Module (IDM) and a Low-Frequency Constraint Module (LFCM). Specifically, IDM reformulates adversarial optimization over diffusion trajectories as an invertible process, enabling constant-memory backpropagation through on-demand reconstruction of intermediate states instead of storing the full denoising chain. Moreover, LFCM leverages Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to decompose latent variables into low- and high-frequency components, restricting perturbations to semantically stable low-frequency subspaces, thereby improving transferability while preserving visual imperceptibility. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks and diverse model architectures demonstrate that IDATA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in attack success rate, memory efficiency, and visual imperceptibility. These results suggest that IDATA is a promising tool for black-box robustness evaluation of deep visual models. Code is available at https://github.com/colourful-pan/IDATA.

</details>


### [136] [Parcel2Progression: An Anatomy-aware Longitudinal Framework for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08753)
*Madhumitha Venkatesh,Shanawaj S Madarkar,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Parcel2Progression (P2P)，一个基于纵向Transformer框架的阿尔茨海默病(AD)进展预测模型，利用图谱引导的分区编码器将3D MRI扫描分词为解剖学上有意义的分区表征，并通过纵向Transformer整合不规则、任意长度的纵向就诊数据及患者年龄，实现了高效性（线性计算复杂度）与可解释性（分区级解读）的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD进展是一个纵向过程，早期阶段存在微妙的病理线索。然而，计算约束限制了大多数神经影像模型，要么牺牲空间信息，要么限制纵向扫描的数量。现有方法无法充分利用高分辨率、变长T1w结构MRI(4D sMRI)扫描序列，需要一种能够同时处理高分辨率空间信息和任意长度纵向数据的方法。

Method: 提出P2P框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)图谱引导的分区编码器(Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder)：将3D扫描token化为更丰富的解剖学基础表征；(2)纵向Transformer(Longitudinal Transformer)：整合不规则的、任意长度的纵向就诊数据以及患者年龄。相较于朴素的二次复杂度4D ViT，该方法计算复杂度与扫描数量呈线性关系。

Result: P2P在ADNI、AIBL和MIRIAD三个数据集的MCI到AD转换预测和AD vs CN分类任务上均优于先前工作及基线。利用纵向扫描相比单次扫描基线，在AD分类和MCI转换预测任务上分别提升了最高5%和7%的平衡准确率。可解释性分析显示临床上一致的AD和MCI萎缩模式；在合成数据集上验证了异常检测的可靠性；并在额颞叶痴呆等其它神经退行性疾病上测试了泛化能力。

Conclusion: P2P通过分区级token化与纵向Transformer的协同设计，成功突破了高分辨率纵向神经影像分析的计算瓶颈，同时提供了分区特异性可解释性和线性可扩展性，在多个数据集和多种任务上展现出优异的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is a longitudinal process with subtle pathological cues in the early stages. Yet, computational constraints have limited most neuroimaging models to either compromise spatial information or limit the number of longitudinal scans. We aim to overcome this bottleneck and fully leverage high-resolution, variable-length T1w structural MRI (4D sMRI) scan sequences. We introduce Parcel2Progression (P2P), a Longitudinal Transformer Framework which tackles this challenge using an Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder that tokenizes 3D scans into a set of richer anatomically grounded representations. A Longitudinal Transformer then integrates irregular, arbitrary-length longitudinal visits with patient age. This synergy delivers two key advantages: (1) parcel-specific interpretability, and (2) computational tractability for long-term analysis, which scales linearly with the number of scans compared to a naive quadratic 4D ViT cost. P2P outperforms prior works and baselines in both MCI (Mild Cognitive Impairment) to AD conversion prediction and AD vs. CN (Cognitively Normal) classification tasks across ADNI, AIBL, and MIRIAD datasets. Leveraging longitudinal scans boosts performance over single-scan baselines by up to 5% and 7% in balanced accuracy for AD classification and MCI conversion prediction tasks, respectively. Interpretability analysis using parcel saliencies and attention rollouts reveals clinically consistent atrophy patterns in AD and MCI subjects. We also demonstrate the frameworks' reliability in anomaly detection using a synthetic dataset, and test the model's generalizability for other neurodegenerative diseases like Frontotemporal Dementia.

</details>


### [137] [UPolarSQ: Polar Representation Learning for Optic Disc and Peripapillary Atrophy Segmentation and Quantification in Fundus Photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08771)
*Mengxian He,Yunyun sun,Ziyue Gao,Wengkei Lam,Shunyi Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UPolarSQ，一个统一的极坐标域框架，用于近视眼底图像中视盘（OD）和视网膜周围萎缩（PPA）的分割及生物标志物定量。通过将OD中心区域映射至极坐标，利用增强的U-Net（UPolarSeg）进行分割，并从预测的极坐标掩膜中确定性提取临床生物标志物（如视盘形态和PPA宽度相关测量），在内外部队列上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近视引起的后极部重构常伴随视盘变形和PPA，二者是临床相关的结构生物标志物。然而在笛卡尔眼底图像中，PPA常呈现为不规则且部分可见的新月形，紧邻视盘，导致分割碎片化和定量结果依赖后处理，缺乏统一且鲁棒的分割与量化方案。

Method: UPolarSQ首先将OD中心感兴趣区域映射至极坐标，使OD和PPA边界可表示为径向轮廓；随后采用UPolarSeg（U-Net架构）进行分割，该网络集成了径向-角度解耦模块（Radial-Angular-Decoupled Module）和边界感知辅助监督，以建模各向异性极坐标特征与径向边界过渡；最终从预测的极坐标掩膜中确定性提取临床生物标志物（包括视盘形态和PPA宽度相关测量），在共享的几何表征下统一分割与定量。

Result: 在内部和外部队列上的实验表明，UPolarSQ改善了OD/PPA分割性能，并支持可靠的极坐标原生生物标志物估计，适用于近视分析。

Conclusion: UPolarSQ通过极坐标域的统一框架有效解决了近视眼底图像中PPA分割碎片化和定量依赖后处理的问题，为临床近视相关结构分析提供了更可靠的分割与定量工具。

Abstract: Myopia-induced posterior-pole remodeling is frequently accompanied by Optic Disc (OD) deformation and Peripapillary Atrophy (PPA), both of which provide clinically relevant structural biomarkers. In Cartesian fundus images, however, PPA often appears as an irregular and partially visible crescent adjacent to the OD, leading to fragmented segmentation and post-processing-dependent quantification. We propose UPolarSQ, a unified polar-domain framework for OD/PPA segmentation and biomarker quantification in myopic fundus images. UPolarSQ first maps an OD-centered region of interest into polar coordinates, where OD and PPA boundaries can be represented as radial profiles. It then employs UPolarSeg, a U-Net-based segmentation network enhanced with a Radial-Angular-Decoupled Module and boundary-aware auxiliary supervision to model anisotropic polar features and radial boundary transitions. Clinical biomarkers, including disc shape and PPA-width-related measurements, are deterministically extracted from the predicted polar masks, aligning segmentation and quantification within a shared geometric representation. Experiments on internal and external cohorts demonstrate that UPolarSQ improves OD/PPA segmentation and supports reliable polar-native biomarker estimation for myopic analysis.

</details>


### [138] [LASA: Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment for Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08805)
*Jinhong Zhu,Weiqi Yan,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LASA（Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment）框架，用于领域泛化语义分割（DGSS）。该框架通过三个协同组件（TSGST、DAQA、DADO）分别解决现有方法中风格随机化导致的流形扭曲和特征归一化导致的判别性细节抑制问题，在多个基准上显著超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DGSS方法（如风格随机化和特征归一化）虽能缓解域偏移，但存在缺陷：风格随机化因粗粒度特性扭曲底层特征流形，特征归一化因刚性设计抑制具有判别性的域敏感语义细节，从而损害特征完整性。

Method: 提出LASA框架，包含三个协同组件：(1) TSGST（文本与源引导风格迁移）：以源特征为结构锚点、以VLM先验为细粒度指导，解决流形扭曲问题；(2) DAQA（域感知查询适配器）：通过类别引导和域感知签名重新校准目标查询，恢复被抑制的判别性和域敏感细节；(3) DADO（域感知解码器优化器）：将查询分布与共享分类器对齐，确保跨域的类别响应一致性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准上进行大量实验，LASA方法显著优于现有最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: LASA框架通过源特征与VLM先验双重锚定的风格迁移、域感知查询校准以及解码器分布对齐，有效解决了传统DGSS方法损害特征完整性的问题，实现了跨域语义分割性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Domain Generalization Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) focuses on generalizing knowledge from labeled source domains to unseen target domains where data is unavailable during the training phase. While conventional methods utilize style randomization or feature normalization to mitigate domain shifts, they often impair feature integrity. Specifically, style randomization distorts the underlying feature manifold due to its coarse-grained nature, while feature normalization suppresses discriminative, domain-sensitive semantic details owing to its rigid design. To address these limitations, we propose the Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment (LASA) framework, which comprises three synergistic components: Text-and-Source-Guided Style Transfer (TSGST), Domain-Aware Query Adapter (DAQA), and Domain-Aware Decoder Optimizer (DADO). Concretely, the TSGST module addresses manifold distortion by utilizing source features as structural anchors and vision-language model (VLM) priors as fine-grained guidance. To restore suppressed discriminative and domain-sensitive details, the DAQA module recalibrates object queries via categorical guidance and domain-aware signatures, while the DADO module aligns the resulting query distributions with a shared classifier to ensure consistent categorical responses across domains. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [139] [AdapterMoE: A Two-Stage Hard-Routing Mixture-of-Experts Architecture for Multi-Crop Disease Recognition with Calibrated Rejection and Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08808)
*Pin-Hsun Huang,Shaou-Gang Miaou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdapterMoE，一种基于确定性硬路由的作物病害识别框架，通过路由头和冻结主干上的每作物适配器，在保持精度与最佳基线无显著差异的同时，将训练成本降至全网络基线的约9%，并提供按作物扩展的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软路由MoE存在专家崩溃（少数专家主导）、与作物无语义对应、重新训练成本高、对非目标输入拒绝不稳定以及精度天花板饱和等问题；本文旨在将目标从单纯追求精度转向部署成本、扩展灵活性与拒绝稳定性之间的权衡。

Method: 采用确定性硬路由方案。RouterHead负责作物分类，并通过最大softmax概率阈值拒绝非目标作物；双门控Energy+KNN的OOD模块捕获分布偏移输入；在冻结的EfficientNet-B0骨干上为每种作物配置5个适配器进行病害判别，每个适配器通过温度缩放校准。由于专家在数据层面硬隔离，避免了专家崩溃，并提供add_crop接口支持局部、按作物的增量更新而非全量重训练。

Result: 在PlantVillage数据集（5种作物、26个类别）上，与五种系统的公平对比中，AdapterMoE的Macro-F1落在最佳基线0.24个百分点的区间内，统计上与最佳基线无显著差异，而训练成本仅约为全网络基线的9%，并支持扩展到新作物。

Conclusion: AdapterMoE证明在作物病害识别中，确定性硬路由可以在不牺牲精度的前提下显著降低训练成本、提高扩展灵活性和拒绝稳定性，是一种更契合实际部署需求的MoE替代方案。

Abstract: Timely crop-disease identification is critical to food security. Multi-crop recognition suits Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), but conventional soft-routing MoE learns crop assignment freely end-to-end, letting a few experts dominate (expert collapse) with no semantic correspondence to crops, and facing high retraining costs, unstable rejection of non-target inputs, and a saturated accuracy ceiling. We shift the objective from accuracy toward a trade-off among deployment cost, scaling flexibility, and rejection stability, using deterministic hard routing. We propose AdapterMoE: a RouterHead classifies the crop and rejects non-target crops via a Maximum Softmax Probability threshold, with a dual-gate Energy+KNN out-of-distribution module catching distribution-shifted inputs; five per-crop Adapters atop a frozen EfficientNet-B0 backbone discriminate diseases, each calibrated via Temperature Scaling. Because experts are hard-isolated at the data level, the design avoids expert collapse and exposes an add_crop interface for local, per-crop updates instead of full retraining. On PlantVillage (5 crops, 26 classes), across a fair five-system comparison, AdapterMoE attains accuracy statistically indistinguishable from the best baselines (Macro-F1 within a 0.24-point band) while cutting training cost to about 9% of full-network baselines, expanding to a new crop in

</details>


### [140] [Multimodal Model Diffing for Feature Discovery and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09928)
*Hunar Batra,Lachin Naghashyar,Ashkan Khakzar,Philip Torr,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Constantin Venhoff,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMDiff，一个多模态模型差异分析框架，通过训练多模态稀疏自编码器（SAEs）并将其转化为特征级接口，实现对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）行为的发现、审计与控制。该框架支持特征隔离、任务特定特征检测和特征级控制三种用途，在LLaVA-MORE、PaliGemma 2和InternVL3.5三个模型家族上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽然展现出强大的视觉理解能力，但其内部特征难以识别、审计或控制。现有的稀疏自编码器（SAEs）分解出的可解释特征方向虽然适用于事后检查，但既不能直接隔离多模态训练改变的特征，也难以用于目标性控制，亟需一个能同时支持特征发现和定向控制的桥梁。

Method: MMDiff通过训练多模态SAEs并将其转化为特征级接口，支持三种用途：(i)特征隔离——对基础语言模型SAE与其多模态适配版本进行差异分析，识别被多模态训练改变的特征；(ii)任务特定特征检测——通过逐token的对比触发分析（contrastive firing analysis）隔离因果特征；(iii)特征级控制——通过因果移除或引导发现的特征方向来控制模型行为。在三个MLLM家族（LLaVA-MORE、PaliGemma 2、InternVL3.5）上训练多模态SAEs。

Result: MMDiff发现稀疏且因果特定的特征：移除这些特征使空间任务性能下降12%、OCR下降17%，多模态安全攻击成功率降低24%，且不影响VQA性能。特征引导在空间和OCR任务上分别比标准单层引导基线平均提升+3.6%和+1.8%的准确率。

Conclusion: 多模态SAEs不仅可作为可解释性工具，更能作为审计、引导和控制MLLM行为的机制，推动更安全、更强大的多模态生成。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong visual understanding, yet the internal features that cause these behaviors remain difficult to identify, audit, or control. While applicable to post-hoc inspection, hidden states that are decomposed into interpretable feature directions using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) neither readily isolate which features are changed by multimodal training, nor are they directly useful for targeted control. We introduce MMDiff, a multimodal model-diffing framework that trains multimodal SAEs and turns them into feature-level interfaces for discovering and controlling multimodal behavior. MMDiff supports three uses: (i) feature isolation, by diffing a base-LM SAE against its multimodal-adapted counterpart to identify features altered by multimodal training; (ii) task-specific feature detection, via per-token contrastive firing analysis that isolates causal features; and (iii) feature-level control, by causally removing or steering the discovered feature directions. We train multimodal SAEs for three MLLM families, LLaVA-MORE, PaliGemma 2, and InternVL3.5, and evaluate on visual-spatial understanding, multimodal safety, and OCR. MMDiff discovers sparse, causally specific features whose removal selectively degrades target behaviors by an average of 12% on spatial tasks and 17% on OCR, and reduces attack success rate by 24% on multimodal safety attacks, with no impact on VQA performance. Steering these features improves spatial and OCR accuracy by +3.6% and +1.8% on average over a standard single-layer steering baseline. These results show that multimodal SAEs can serve not only as interpretability tools, but as mechanisms for auditing, steering, and controlling MLLMs behavior toward safer and more capable generations.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Unified Hallucination Fuzzing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07525)
*Pengfei Zhou,Jiajun Song,Zhiwei Tang,Yixing Ma,Xiaopeng Peng,Donghui Si,Yuhang Xu,Huiqi Song,Yiyuan Miao,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Wangbo Zhao,Bohan Zhuang,Jiasheng Tang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统性的多模态大模型幻觉评估框架，包含细粒度基准UniHall和自适应的多模态模糊测试（SAMF），揭示了SOTA模型在模糊测试下性能显著退化，并发现了"有帮助性-幻觉"权衡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准评估存在分类覆盖范围狭窄和性能饱和的问题，难以反映模型在真实动态场景中的鲁棒性，无法有效评估多模态大模型的幻觉问题。

Method: 首先构建基于统一分类法（涵盖Object、Instruction、Knowledge三个维度）的细粒度基准UniHall；其次提出自适应的SAMF框架，采用进化变异策略探索模型幻觉边界；最后引入由多模态oracles集成驱动的结构化评估指标套件，确保动态输入评估的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明SOTA多模态大模型在模糊测试下相比常规设置出现显著性能退化，暴露了推理能力与事实基础之间的脱节；同时发现强化学习对齐会加剧指令跟随任务中的谄媚行为，形成"有帮助性-幻觉"权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合细粒度基准与自适应压力测试，弥补了静态评估的不足，为多模态大模型幻觉的可靠评估提供了新范式，相关框架、代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), severely limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Existing evaluations, predominantly based on static benchmarks, suffer from narrow taxonomical coverage and rapid performance saturation, failing to reflect model robustness in evolving real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation framework integrating a comprehensive benchmark with self-evolving stress testing. First, we introduce UniHall, a fine-grained dataset grounded in a unified taxonomy spanning Object, Instruction, and Knowledge dimensions. Second, to address benchmark saturation, we propose Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing (SAMF), a self-adaptive framework that employs evolutionary mutation strategies to explore the boundaries of model hallucinations. Crucially, to ensure reliable assessment of dynamic inputs, SAMF incorporates a structured metric suite driven by an ensemble of multi-modal oracles. Our extensive experiments reveal that state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant performance degradation under fuzzing compared to conventional settings, exposing a dissociation between reasoning capabilities and factual grounding. Furthermore, we identify a helpfulness-hallucination trade-off, where reinforcement learning alignment inadvertently exacerbates sycophancy in instruction-following tasks. The framework, code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/LanceZPF/EvalHall.

</details>


### [142] [DocAtlas: Long-Document Understanding as Mutable-State Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07527)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocAtlas是一个将长文档理解建模为可变状态信息搜索过程的系统，通过外部"文档工具环境"控制搜索、阅读、笔记和回顾等操作，在固定上下文预算下结合自改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆。在MMLongBench-Doc上，GPT-5.4达到71.4%（超过人类专家65.8%），Qwen3.5-4B经端到端强化学习训练后达到63.7%，远超54.4%的直接输入基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强系统通常在生成前从静态索引中选择证据，而智能体系统虽支持多轮工具调用但依赖冻结的商业模型且行为仅由提示词决定，缺乏对长文档理解中跨多页、多布局、多表格和图表证据的灵活组合能力。

Method: DocAtlas将长文档理解构建为"可变文档工具环境"（mutable document harness）：一个外部环境在每个步骤决定哪些文档信息被搜索、读取、存储、回顾和展示给模型。该环境提供搜索、阅读、笔记和回顾工具，维护层次化文档树和笔记存储，并随智能体记录证据而动态更新。系统结合自改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆，在固定上下文预算下运行；同一工具环境既支持大型VLM的推理时使用，也支持紧凑VLM智能体的端到端强化学习训练。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc基准上，DocAtlas配合GPT-5.4达到71.4%的准确率，超过65.8%的人类专家参考水平；使用Qwen3.5-4B这一紧凑VLM在DocAtlas环境中进行端到端强化学习训练后达到63.7%，相比54.4%的直接输入基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: 可变文档工具环境的设计能够大幅提升紧凑文档智能体的性能，表明将文档理解过程外部化、环境化（而非仅依赖静态检索或提示驱动的行为）是一种有效的系统设计范式。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires models to find and combine evidence across many pages, layouts, tables, figures, and charts. Existing retrieval-augmented systems usually select evidence from a static index before generation, while recent agentic systems add multi-turn tool use but often rely on frozen proprietary backbones whose behavior is set by prompts. We present DocAtlas, a system that treats long-document understanding as a mutable-state information-seeking process. We instantiate DocAtlas as a mutable document harness: an external environment that determines what document information is searched, read, stored, reviewed, and shown to the model at each step. Given a document and question, the harness exposes search, reading, note-taking, and review tools, maintains a hierarchical tree and note store, and updates both as the agent records evidence. DocAtlas combines self-improving retrieval, selective evidence access, and active working memory under a fixed context budget. The same harness supports inference-time use with large VLMs and end-to-end reinforcement learning for compact VLM agents. With GPT-5.4, DocAtlas reaches 71.4\% on MMLongBench-Doc, exceeding the human-expert reference of 65.8\%. A Qwen3.5-4B VLM trained with end-to-end RL in the DocAtlas environment reaches 63.7\%, compared with a 54.4\% direct-input baseline, showing that mutable document-harness design can improve compact document agents by a large margin.

</details>


### [143] [WuYuEval: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Large Language Models in Solid Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07529)
*Yi Zhang,Hongyang Wang,Zheng Hao Leong,Zihao Wu,Kaijun Lin,Zhixing Pan,Qixun Huangfu,Wei Ren,Wenyan Wu,Fangyun Wang,Wenting Yu,Hengyu Lin,Muling Yang,Zongguo Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了WuYuEval，一个面向固体废物管理领域的多层级基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在基础知识、领域推理和专家决策三个层次的专业能力。基准包含基础模块（4,590道多项选择题）和专家模块（247道场景开放式问题），对33个LLM的评估显示性能差异显著，且推理型思维模式并非总是有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重于通用知识而非专业决策，无法衡量LLM在工程、环境和政策约束下的固体废物管理能力，因此需要构建一个专业领域的多层级评估基准来更准确地衡量LLM的SWM能力。

Method: 构建了WuYuEval双模块基准：基础模块包含6种任务类型和8个领域类别的4,590道封闭式多项选择题；专家模块包含247道涉及多目标优化、约束权衡和系统设计的场景开放式问题。专家任务采用锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge评分结合Elo成对比较方法，并对33个LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 最优模型在基础模块达到94.64%准确率，但平均准确率从简单题的84.14%降至难题的42.50%；表现较低的集中在计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放式专家任务。推理型思维模式在审计后改善了多数匹配模型对，但收益依赖于基线能力且并非一致正向。

Conclusion: 可见的推理（Thinking模式）仅在锚定于单位、假设和工程约束时才有帮助，否则可能偏离关键答案边界。WuYuEval为开发具有专业推理链和显式约束控制的SWM领域基础模型提供了评估资源和实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as technical assistants, but their competence in solid waste management (SWM) remains difficult to assess because existing benchmarks emphasize general knowledge rather than professional decisions under engineering, environmental, and policy constraints. We introduce WuYuEval, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating LLMs in SWM across foundational knowledge, domain reasoning, and expert decision-making. After quality auditing, WuYuEval contains a Foundation Module with 4,590 closed-ended multiple-choice questions across six task types and eight domain categories, together with an Expert Module with 247 scenario-based open-ended questions involving multi-objective optimization, constraint trade-offs, and system design. For expert tasks, we combine anchor-calibrated LLM-as-a-Judge scoring with Elo-based pairwise comparison. Across 33 LLMs, performance varied widely. The leading model reached 94.64\% accuracy on the Foundation Module, but average accuracy still fell from 84.14\% on easy questions to 42.50\% on hard questions, with lower performance concentrated in calculation, experimental design, urban planning, and open-ended expert tasks. Reasoning-oriented Thinking modes improve most matched model pairs after auditing, but the gains depend on baseline capability and are not uniformly positive. These results suggest that visible deliberation helps only when it remains anchored to units, assumptions, and engineering constraints; otherwise, it may drift from decisive answer boundaries. WuYuEval therefore provides both an evaluation resource and an empirical basis for developing SWM-oriented foundation models with professional reasoning chains and explicit constraint control.

</details>


### [144] [Search-G1: Grounded Search Agents via Representation-Based Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07531)
*Cheng Ruoxi,Ma Haoxuan,Zhang Hongyi,Zhang Junming,Duan Ranjie,Xia Qiaolin,Wang Hao,Lu Yu,Shi Haibo,Ma Xingjun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Search-G1，一种基于表征的内在奖励框架，通过两种干预校准的读数（提示状态读数和答案承诺读数）评估智能体回答的操作性接地，无需过程标注或LLM评判推理即可优化搜索增强语言智能体的检索-接地权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部奖励要么只提供稀疏的结果监督，要么需要昂贵的过程标注或LLM评判推理；而基于策略侧信号（如熵、似然、信息增益）的内部奖励虽然廉价且分级，但主要反映模型置信度而非证据接地。需要一种既低成本又能区分有无必要检索与证据接地的奖励机制。

Method: Search-G1采用两个干预校准的读数：(1) 提示状态读数预测闭卷充分性，其补集定义策略相对的检索必要性；(2) 答案承诺读数通过删除证据来估计答案阶段的证据依赖度。两者的结合为正确的已检索轨迹（当检索被估计为必要时且答案对证据敏感）提供额外信用，在闭卷知识足够时奖励直接回答，并惩罚重复检索。由于RL会改变策略表征，Search-G1在最新检查点的轨迹上周期性地重新拟合两种读数，使奖励随策略共同演化。

Result: 在多个基于检索的问答基准和两种模型规模上的实验表明，Search-G1改善了接地-搜索成本权衡，在保持竞争力的任务准确率的同时产生更短的响应侧轨迹。

Conclusion: Search-G1证明基于表征的内在奖励框架能够以低成本实现有效的检索必要性和证据接地评估，通过周期性重拟合保证奖励与策略的协同演化，在搜索型问答任务中取得了更好的接地与搜索成本平衡。

Abstract: Search-augmented language agents should retrieve external information only when necessary and ground their answers in retrieved evidence. Existing external rewards provide either sparse outcome supervision or richer feedback from process annotations and LLM judges. Outcome rewards scale readily but cannot distinguish grounded retrieval from redundant search, whereas richer signals require costly annotation or inference during training. Internal rewards based on policy-side signals such as entropy, likelihood, or information gain are graded and inexpensive to evaluate, yet mainly reflect model confidence rather than evidence grounding. We propose Search-G1, a representation-based intrinsic reward framework that measures the operational grounding of an agent's answers through two intervention-calibrated readouts. A prompt-state readout predicts closed-book sufficiency, whose complement defines policy-relative retrieval necessity; an answer-commit readout estimates evidence reliance from answer-stage sensitivity to evidence deletion. Together, they provide additional credit to correct searched trajectories when retrieval is estimated necessary and the answer is evidence-sensitive, favor correct direct answers when closed-book knowledge suffices, and penalize repeated search. After calibration, reward scoring requires neither process annotations nor LLM-as-judge inference during policy optimization. Because reinforcement learning changes policy representations, Search-G1 periodically refits both readouts on trajectories from the latest checkpoint, allowing the reward to co-evolve with the policy. Experiments across multiple search-based question-answering benchmarks and two model scales show that Search-G1 improves the grounding--search-cost trade-off, producing shorter response-side trajectories at competitive task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/Rosy0912/Search-G1.

</details>


### [145] [Scaling Inherently Interpretable Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07594)
*Guide Labs Team,Andreas Madsen,Aya Abdelsalam Ismail,Giang Nguyen,Isaac Plant,Muawiz Chaudhary,Nathaniel Monson,Saqib Azim,Zhichen Guo,Julius Adebayo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战"可解释性是能力的代价"这一传统假设，提出将可解释性作为训练管线中的约束条件与语言建模目标共同优化。在三个数量级的计算规模上，无论自回归还是扩散语言模型，可解释性都随能力提升而增强。作者构建了Steerling-8B扩散语言模型，支持端到端的归因与闭环干预，且性能与训练计算量高出2-16倍的开源模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法是在模型训练完成后进行事后逆向工程，其可靠性难以验证。作者希望从根本上改变这一范式，将可解释性从训练后的"事后补救"转变为训练过程中的"内置约束"，从而打破可解释性与能力之间的权衡关系。

Method: 将可解释性作为训练目标的一部分与语言建模目标联合优化；在自回归和扩散两类语言模型上、跨三个数量级的计算规模进行验证；具体实现为Steerling-8B——一个带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，能够将输出归因于输入token、人类可理解的概念以及训练数据。

Result: 可解释性随模型规模扩展而提升而非退化；模型表征变得更加解耦且与人类可理解概念对齐。Steerling-8B在性能上与使用2-16倍更多计算量训练的开源模型相当。该模型支持闭环干预：通过概念/特征归因诊断输出、检索相似训练数据、并通过概念引导（steering）纠正行为而无需重训。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以被设计进训练过程本身，且随模型规模增长而改善——这代表了一种不同的扩展范式。该工作表明解释性与能力可以共同扩展，而非相互权衡，为未来可解释AI模型的训练范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: Interpretability is often treated as a tax on capability: language models are trained as opaque systems, then explained after the fact, with methods whose reliability is difficult to establish. In this work, we challenge this premise. Rather than reverse-engineering a model, we make interpretability a constraint of the training pipeline, optimized alongside the language modeling objective. Across three orders of magnitude of compute, on both autoregressive and diffusion language models, interpretability scales with capability rather than against it. Surprisingly, model representations become more disentangled and aligned with human-understandable concepts with scale.
  We instantiate the training-time recipe with Steerling-8B, a diffusion language model with a causal attention mask. For any group of generated tokens, Steerling-8B attributes the output to relevant input tokens, human-understandable concepts, and training data. This enables closed-loop intervention: diagnose an output through its concept or feature attribution, retrieve similar training data, and correct the behavior through concept steering without retraining. Steerling-8B remains competitive with open peer models trained on substantially 2-16x more compute, suggesting a different scaling paradigm: interpretability can be designed into training, and it improves with scale.

</details>


### [146] [Embedding Initialization for Unseen Low-resource Languages in Multilingual NMT: A Case Study on Limbum-English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07629)
*Samiratu Ntohsi,Neza David Tuyishimire,Anesu Kafesu,Marvin Ogore,Samuel Oluwajunwonlo Babalola,Oche Ankeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多语言神经机器翻译模型（如NLLB-200）不支持大多数喀麦隆Grassfields Bantu语言的问题，提出了一种语言token嵌入初始化策略：用多个类型学相关语言嵌入的平均值来初始化新语言的token。在Limbum-to-English翻译任务上（8,837个句对），该方法达到chrF2++=46.7，与最佳单语言代理（Swahili，chrF2++=47.3）相当，且远远优于零样本（12.5）和从头训练（14.5）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLLB-200覆盖200种语言，但仍有数千种语言不受支持，包括大多数喀麦隆Grassfields Bantu语言。当针对未支持语言微调模型时，实践者必须选择代理语言token，但缺乏有原则性的选择方法。本文旨在消除这种启发式代理选择的必要性。

Method: 提出一种嵌入初始化策略：新语言token的嵌入初始化为模型中多个类型学相关语言嵌入的平均值。在NLLB-200上对Limbum-to-English翻译进行微调，使用来自《新约》文本和双语词典的8,837个平行句对作为训练数据。

Result: 平均嵌入初始化方法chrF2++=46.7，接近最佳单语言代理（Swahili，47.3），远超零样本（12.5）和从头训练Transformer（14.5）。两种NLLB-200变体均比从头训练基线提高超过32个chrF2++点。但所有系统均未能保留声调变音符号。

Conclusion: 多语言迁移是极低资源Bantu语言翻译中的主导因素，同时该嵌入初始化方法消除了启发式代理选择的需要。然而，声调变音符号的缺失仍是待解决的开放挑战。数据集和代码已公开发布以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multilingual neural machine translation models such as NLLB-200 cover 200 languages but leave thousands unsupported, including most Grassfields Bantu languages of Cameroon. When fine-tuning these models for an unseen language, practitioners must choose a proxy language token, yet no principled method exists for this selection. We implemented an embedding initialization strategy where a language token is the average of embeddings from multiple typologically related languages already in the mod el. We evaluate this approach on Limbum-to-English translation using a parallel corpus of 8,837 sentence pairs from New Testament text and a bilingual dictionary. We compare models: NLLB-200 zero-shot (chrF2++ = 12.5), a Transformer trained from scratch (chrF2++ = 14.5), NLLB-200 fine-tuned with a Swahili proxy token (chrF2++ = 47.3), and NLLB-200 with our averaged embedding initialization (chrF2++ = 46.7). We find that the multi-language initialization achieves performance comparable to the best single-language proxy. Both NLLB-200 variants improve over the from-scratch baseline by over 32 chrF2++ points. These results show that multilingual transfer is the dominant factor in extremely low-resource Bantu translation while eliminating the need for heuristic proxy selection. However, all systems fail to preserve tonal diacritics, highlighting an open challenge. We make our dataset and code available to support further research.

</details>


### [147] [SurveyReview: A Reviewer-Aligned Benchmark for Survey Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07641)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yuanchun Wang,Fanjin Zhang,Ruyu Zhao,Juanzi Li,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SurveyReview，一个与人类审稿人对齐的多维度综述论文评估基准和数据集，包含675篇综述论文和1,630份审稿报告，将自由文本审稿评论转化为四个维度的评分（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）。同时提出SurveyAlign基线评估器，通过LoRA微调Qwen3-32B并结合外部知识，将平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69，显著提升了与人类审稿人的对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型将综述写作自动化，可靠的评估成为瓶颈。现有LLM-as-a-judge方法缺乏与人类审稿人的系统对齐，且缺少量化这种对齐程度的系统框架。

Method: （1）构建SurveyReview基准：收集并标注675篇综述论文和1,630份审稿报告，将自由形式的同行评审评论转化为四维评分（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）并附带支持性理由；（2）发布标准化训练/测试划分和评估协议，用于衡量自动评估器与人类审稿人的对齐度；（3）开发SurveyAlign基线评估器：使用LoRA对Qwen3-32B在标注数据上进行微调，并针对知识密集型维度增强外部知识。

Result: 在测试集上，SurveyAlign相比基于GPT-5.2的提示式评判，显著提升了与人类审稿人的对齐度，四个维度的平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69。

Conclusion: 本文的两方面贡献：（1）建立了首个多维度、审稿人对齐的综述评审数据集及可复现的评估框架；（2）开发了与人类审稿人高度对齐的强基线评估器，为未来研究提供了竞争性参考。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models has transformed survey writing from a months-long manual effort into an automated process. As generation scales, reliable evaluation becomes the bottleneck, and LLMs are increasingly used as survey evaluators. However, existing approaches largely rely on off-the-shelf LLM-as-a-judge methods without systematic alignment to human reviewers, and there remains a lack of systematic frameworks for quantifying alignment with human reviewers. To address this gap, we propose SurveyReview, a reviewer-aligned, multi-dimensional benchmark and dataset for survey evaluation. We collect and annotate 675 survey papers with 1,630 review reports. We structure authentic peer-review reports by converting free-form comments into four-dimensional scores (Readability, Criticalness, Comprehensiveness, Structure) paired with supporting rationales. We further release standardized train/test splits and an evaluation protocol to measure alignment between automatic evaluators and human reviewers. To validate the benchmark, we develop SurveyAlign, a strong baseline evaluator by fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with LoRA on our annotated data, augmented with external knowledge for knowledge-intensive dimensions. On the test set, SurveyAlign substantially improves reviewer alignment over prompt-based judging with GPT-5.2, reducing average MSE from 2.28 to 1.38 and MAE from 1.15 to 0.69 across all four dimensions. Our contributions are twofold: (1) we establish the first multi-dimensional, reviewer-aligned dataset with a reproducible evaluation framework for survey reviewing; (2) we develop a strong baseline evaluator that substantially improves alignment with human reviewers, providing a competitive reference for future research. Our code and data are available at https://surveyreview.github.io

</details>


### [148] [Evaluating Dedicated Monolingual and Joint Multilingual Causal Models for Dravidian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07727)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文训练了5个GPT-2架构的模型，比较4个达罗毗荼语系（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语）的独立单语模型与1个共享分词器的多语模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达罗毗荼语系语言在多语语言模型的训练数据中占比很小，因此这些模型对这些语言的实际能力不明确，需要系统评估单语模型与多语模型在低资源语言上的表现差异。

Method: 从零训练5个GPT-2架构模型：4个单语模型（每种语言配32K词表子词分词器）和1个共享64K词表子词分词器的多语模型。所有模型使用清洗后的CC-100、Wikipedia和Samanantar数据训练，并从困惑度、每字节比特数、分词器效率和微调结果四个维度与mGPT对比评估。

Result: 单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别任务上优于mGPT，且在所有测试语言上，单语分词器的效率均高于共享的多语分词器。

Conclusion: 对于达罗毗荼语系这类在多语模型数据占比少的低资源语言，采用单语模型和专属分词器能获得更好的性能和更高的分词效率。

Abstract: Dravidian languages, mainly Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam make up only a small part of the data used to train multilingual language models, so it's not clear how much per-language ability these models actually keep. I have trained five GPT-2 architecture models from scratch to compare four monolingual models (one each for Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam, each with its own 32K-vocabulary subword tokenizer) against one multilingual model sharing a 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizer across all four languages. All the 5 models are trained on cleaned CC-100, Wikipedia, and Samanantar data. I have tested the models on perplexity, bits-per-byte, tokenizer efficiency, and fine-tuning results which are compared against mGPT. The monolingual models outperform mGPT on sentiment classification and named entity recognition, and their tokenizers proved more efficient than the shared multilingual model across all the languages tested.

</details>


### [149] [The No-Meaning Falsity: The Structural Impossibility of the Arbitrary Sign in Classical Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07737)
*Elnaserledinellah Mahmoud Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过建立古典阿拉伯语非连接形态学的形式数学模型，证明了后现代主义关于语义无限不确定性的主张与索绪尔任意符号公理在阿拉伯语中不成立，并利用条件Kolmogorov复杂度形式化"相对任意性"，证明阿拉伯语相对任意性是可判定的且小于1，从而在系统复杂度上与印欧语系形成严格不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后现代主义主张语义无限不确定性，其理论基础是索绪尔的任意符号公理。本文旨在检验这一主张是否与古典阿拉伯语的结构架构兼容，并为"相对任意性"提供严格的形式化定义和数学证明。

Method: 本文构建了阿拉伯语非连接形态学的形式数学模型，其中词汇意义由不变词根与形态句法模式的交互决定。基于此框架，建立了形态对应定理（证明每个词汇项由词根-模式对唯一生成）和语义定位定理（证明词汇意义在派生层面先于表面实现被确定）。同时，利用条件Kolmogorov复杂度将索绪尔的相对任意性形式化为算法上的"无规则性质"，并通过可判定性与不可判定性证明来支撑论证。

Result: 证明了普适的相对任意性在形式上是不可判定的，而阿拉伯语的相对任意性是可判定的，且对于其被驱动的能指（W层和M层）被证明严格小于1，从而确立了阿拉伯语与印欧语系之间的系统复杂度严格不对称性。

Conclusion: 后现代主义的语义无限不确定性主张与索绪尔任意符号公理在古典阿拉伯语的结构架构下不成立；阿拉伯语的形态系统展现了可判定的、受约束的相对任意性，这在系统复杂度层面与印欧语系形成严格不对称，为自然语言任意性的形式化研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether the postmodern claim of unrestricted semantic indeterminacy, and its foundational Saussurean axiom of the arbitrary sign, are compatible with the structural architecture of Classical Arabic. We develop a formal mathematical model of Arabic non concatenative morphology in which lexical meaning is determined by the interaction between an invariant root and a morphosyntactic pattern. Within this framework, we establish a Morphological Correspondence Theorem, demonstrating that every lexical item is uniquely generated by a root pattern pair, and a Semantic Localization Theorem, proving that lexical meaning is determined at the derivational level prior to surface realization. To address Saussurean weaker notion of relative arbitrariness, we formalize it via conditional Kolmogorov complexity, defining arbitrariness algorithmically as the no rule property. We prove that general relative arbitrariness is formally undecidable, while Arabic relative arbitrariness is decidable and provably less than 1 for its motivated signifiers (Levels W and M), establishing a strict system complexity asymmetry over Indo-European languages.

</details>


### [150] [Jako Tako or Fluent? Presenting PoVisLE: A Polish Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07763)
*Anna Kołos,Grzegorz Statkiewicz,Karolina Seweryn,Katarzyna Kowol,Karolina Piosek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PoVisLE，一个面向波兰语的单文化视觉语言基准数据集，包含1,117张图像和2,366个手工标注的VQA问答对，在"接地评估"范式下评测模型对文化嵌入式多模态理解的能力，超越了浅层识别层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）主要基于英语中心化数据进行训练，在处理文化特定的视觉理解时表现不佳，无法理解区域特定含义、象征内容及上下文相关的视觉线索。现有的文化能力基准多为模板驱动且聚焦表面识别，不足以评估更深层的语言和语用理解能力。

Method: 构建PoVisLE单文化基准数据集，包含1,117张图像和2,366个手工标注的VQA问答对；采用"接地评估"（grounded evaluation）范式，即在视觉语境交互中解释语言，而非孤立的表层识别。

Result: 该数据集提供了一个受控且具有挑战性的资源，用于评估超越表面识别的文化嵌入式视觉语言理解能力，填补了现有基准在深层文化和语用理解评估方面的空白。

Conclusion: PoVisLE为评估多模态模型在文化情境下的深层理解能力提供了新的基准资源，强调语言与视觉语境交互的重要性，推动VLM向更真实、更接地气的文化理解方向发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on tasks such as image captioning, visual question answering, and image-to-text generation. However, they are predominantly trained on English-centric data, which limits their ability to handle culturally grounded visual understanding and leads to failures in interpreting region-specific meanings, symbolic content, and context-dependent visual cues. Existing benchmarks for cultural competence are often template-driven and focused on surface-level recognition, making them insufficient for evaluating deeper linguistic and pragmatic understanding in culturally situated settings. We introduce PoVisLE, a monocultural vision-language benchmark for Polish designed to evaluate culturally grounded multimodal understanding under a grounded evaluation paradigm, where language is interpreted in interaction with visual context. The dataset contains 1,117 images and 2,366 manually annotated VQA pairs. Overall, our dataset provides a controlled and challenging resource for assessing culturally grounded vision-language understanding beyond surface-level recognition.

</details>


### [151] [On the use of foundation models in cognitive science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07812)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Alex Warstadt,Michael Frank,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四阶段推理框架，用于评估基础模型（FMs）作为认知与发展模型的科学有效性，强调仅凭行为对齐不足以建立解释性模型地位，必须嵌入明确的理论承诺、理论诊断性任务和对比性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来大量研究评估了基础模型（FMs）在认知与发展维度上与人类行为的一致性，但将FMs用作候选认知模型存在重大的方法论和概念挑战。核心问题在于：在什么条件下，行为对齐才能证明将FMs视为认知的解释性模型是合理的？现有研究缺乏系统化的评估框架。

Method: 作者提出一个四阶段推理框架：（1）将人类实验任务适配为模型可兼容的格式；（2）明确链接假设（linking hypotheses），将模型输出映射到人类行为测量指标；（3）评估行为对应性；（4）在候选模型或操作之间进行对比评估。文章同时阐述了链接假设在映射模型输出与人类行为测量中的作用，识别了限制对齐声明有效性的挑战，并提出了理论驱动与对比性评估的原则。

Result: 文章论证了行为拟合本身不足以证明FMs作为认知模型的科学有效性。只有当行为对齐被嵌入到明确的理论承诺、理论诊断性任务以及系统化的候选模型对比评估之中时，对齐才具有科学意义。该框架为认知和发展建模中FMs的严谨使用提供了方法论指导。

Conclusion: 行为对齐只有在明确的理论基础上才有解释力。研究者应将FMs的评估纳入四阶段推理框架，通过链接假设的明确化、理论诊断性任务的设计以及跨模型的系统对比，才能确保行为对齐转化为有意义的认知科学洞见。

Abstract: A host of recent studies have evaluated the cognitive and developmental alignment of Foundation Models (FMs). These investigations include evaluations of their correspondence to adult performance across a range of cognitive domains, as well as whether aspects of model training track children's cognitive development. However, using FMs as candidate cognitive models poses significant methodological and conceptual challenges. A key question underlies this effort: under what conditions does behavioral alignment justify treating FMs as explanatory models of cognition? In this paper, we articulate a four-stage inferential framework for evaluating FMs as cognitive and developmental models: adapting human experimental tasks to model-compatible formats, specifying linking hypotheses that map model outputs to human measures, evaluating behavioral correspondence, and comparing across candidate models or manipulations. We clarify the role of linking hypotheses in mapping model outputs to human behavioral measures, identify challenges that constrain alignment claims, and propose principles for theory-driven and comparative evaluation. Throughout, we argue that behavioral fit alone is insufficient. Alignment becomes scientifically meaningful only when embedded within explicit theoretical commitments, theory-diagnostic tasks, and systematic contrastive evaluation across candidate models.

</details>


### [152] ["Many Are My Names": The Anatomy of the Assistant and Its Personas via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07852)
*Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型内部如何表示不同说话者（Assistant、角色扮演人格、叙述故事角色），通过稀疏自编码器提取特征，发现Assistant和角色扮演人格共享同一特征核心，而故事角色则缺乏该核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何内部表征"谁在说话"（助手、角色扮演人格或叙述故事角色）这一问题尚未得到充分探索，理解这一点对于可解释性和模型控制至关重要。

Method: 使用用户表达情绪的文本及对应模型响应数据集，将三种生成场景（Assistant、Roleplay、Story）分解为稀疏自编码器特征，在回合边界和代词token位置提取特征，通过过滤管线选择不同深度，并通过操控效应和激活分布刻画每个存活特征。

Result: 主要发现：Assistant与角色扮演人格并非独立替代关系——人格保留Assistant关联的特征核心，并在各层逐步分化（从操作机制到行为与风格特征）；而生成的故事角色缺乏Assistant关联核心。Story和Roleplay均可通过沉浸式模拟模式与Assistant区分，但Assistant在默认设置下有时也会进入或缓慢漂移到该模式。

Conclusion: 语言模型内部存在一个"Assistant核心"特征基，角色扮演人格在此基础上渐进分化，而故事角色则独立于该核心构建；沉浸式模拟模式是区分说话者的关键机制，但其边界可能模糊。

Abstract: How a language model internally represents who is speaking, the Assistant, an assigned roleplay persona, or a narrated story character, remains underexplored. We study speaker representations using a dataset of user-expressed emotional text and corresponding model responses. We decompose three generation settings (Assistant, Roleplay, and Story) into sparse autoencoder features extracted at turn-boundary and pronoun-token positions and selected through a filtering pipeline for different depths. We characterize each surviving feature through its steering effects and activation distribution. Our main finding is that the Assistant and roleplay personas are not independent alternatives: personas retain the Assistant-associated feature core while progressively differentiating from it across layers, starting from operational machinery towards behavioral and stylistic features. Meanwhile, generated story characters lack the Assistant-associated core. Both Story and Roleplay can be distinguished from the Assistant with Immersive Simulation Mode. However, the Assistant can sometimes enter or slowly drift into it even in the default setting.

</details>


### [153] [SurakshaEval: An Indic Safety Benchmark for Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07862)
*Debopriyo Banerjee,Kapil Rajesh Kavitha,Angana Borah,Xudong Han,Yuxia Wang,Parameswari Krishnamurthy,Utkarsh Agarwal,Atharva Kulkarni,Swaran Lata,Ayush Munot,Dhruv Sahnan,Aaryamonvikram Singh,Preslav Nakov,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SurakshaEval 是一个专为十种主要印度语言（阿萨姆语、孟加拉语、古吉拉特语、印地语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语、马拉地语、旁遮普语、泰米尔语和泰卢固语）以及英语设计的新型安全基准测试集，包含人工撰写、覆盖真实场景的提示词以及区域和语言特定的文化敏感提示词。研究结果表明，即便是强大的多语言大语言模型在印度语言（尤其是本地文字）环境中也难以可靠满足细致的安全要求，存在过度拒绝、隐性偏见检测缺失和区域敏感情境中上下文意识不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全评估数据集主要聚焦于英语和西方语境，忽视了其他语言中的语言多样性和文化根植的安全风险。为填补这一空白，研究者提出了面向印度语言的全面安全基准。

Method: 构建了包含通用印度提示词及区域/语言特定提示词的安全基准，涵盖十种印度语言和英语；在多种最先进的大语言模型上进行基准测试，建立安全基线性能，并分析失效模式。

Result: 强多语言LLMs在印度语言环境（尤其本地文字）中难以满足细致安全要求；识别出过度拒绝、隐性偏见遗漏和区域敏感情境中上下文意识不足三类主要失效模式。

Conclusion: 亟需纳入区域特定数据和结构化评估协议的安全评估框架，以支持AI系统在不同社会价值观下安全、道德地部署。

Abstract: Existing safety evaluation datasets for large language models (LLMs) predominantly focus on English and Western contexts, often overlooking the linguistic diversity and culturally grounded safety risks present in other languages. To address this gap, we introduce SurakshaEval, a novel safety benchmark composed of human-written prompts spanning real-world scenarios, explicitly designed for ten major Indian languages - Assamese, Bengali, Gujarati, Hindi, Kannada, Malayalam, Marathi, Punjabi, Tamil, and Telugu, along with English. SurakshaEval includes both generic prompts common across India and region- and language-specific prompts that capture localized sociocultural sensitivities. We benchmark a broad range of state-of-the-art LLMs on SurakshaEval, establish baseline safety performance, and identify recurring failure modes, including over-refusal, missed detection of implicit bias, and insufficient contextual awareness in regionally sensitive settings. Our results show that even strong multilingual LLMs struggle to reliably meet nuanced safety requirements when operating in Indic languages, particularly in native scripts. These findings highlight the urgent need for safety evaluation frameworks that incorporate region-specific data and structured assessment protocols, enabling the development and deployment of AI systems that operate securely, ethically, and in alignment with diverse societal values. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/SurakshaEval. Warning: This paper contains text that may be offensive or unsafe.

</details>


### [154] [Detection of Self-Introductions in Legislative Testimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07891)
*Sofija Dimitrijevic,Pallavi Das,Kasey Liu,Foaad Khosmood*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于立法委员会证词中自我介绍的检测与说话人姓名提取流水线，使用机器学习方法，在154万条话语上训练决策树、随机森林和XGBoost三类分类器，并整合多种特征。XGBoost达到最优F1分数0.9747，进一步加入微调BERT概率特征后F1提升至0.9782，测试错误从241降至207。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我介绍在立法委员会证词中非常普遍，成功检测自我介绍并提取说话人姓名对于政府会议场景下的说话人识别任务至关重要，因此需要开发一个自动化的自我介绍检测与姓名提取流水线。

Method: 构建包含154万条话语的五州立法会议训练数据集；通过姓名匹配启发式方法生成自动标签；训练三种分类器（决策树、随机森林、XGBoost）；构建融合词袋、位置上下文、结构信号、介绍短语指示符和语篇上下文特征的特征集；并作为扩展，使用微调BERT分类器对全量候选数据集评分，将BERT概率输出作为附加特征整合进XGBoost模型中。

Result: XGBoost在三个分类器中表现最佳，F1分数为0.9747且总错误数最少；加入BERT概率特征后，F1从0.9747提升至0.9782，总测试错误从241降至207。与决策树基线（F1 0.9323）相比，主要提升来自语篇上下文特征和提升集成策略，BERT提供适度的补充信号。误报分析表明少数误报实为源数据中姓名不一致导致的真实自我介绍误标记，实际性能被略微低估。

Conclusion: 该流水线在立法委员会证词的自我介绍检测与说话人姓名提取任务上取得了优异的性能，其中BERT增强的XGBoost模型表现最佳。语篇上下文特征和集成学习策略是性能提升的主要驱动力，而BERT提供了有价值的补充信号。

Abstract: Self-introductions are common in legislative committee testimonies. Successfully detecting them and extracting the speaker's name is enormously helpful in the task of speaker identification in the context of government meetings. In this paper, we present a pipeline for detection of self-introductions in legislative committee testimony using machine learning. We construct a training dataset from 1.54 million utterances spanning five state legislative sessions, apply a name-matching heuristic to generate automatic labels, and train three classifiers: a decision tree, random forest, and XGBoost to find self-introductions and extract the speaker's name. We construct a feature set combining bag-of-words, positional context, structural signals, introductory phrase indicators, and discourse context features. Among the three classifiers, XGBoost achieves the best performance with an F1 score of 0.9747 and the fewest total errors; adding fine-tuned BERT probability features improves this further. As an extension, we score the full candidate dataset with a fine-tuned BERT classifier and add BERT probability outputs as features. This BERT-augmented XGBoost model improves F1 from 0.9747 to 0.9782 and reduces total test errors from 241 to 207. The primary gain over the decision tree baseline (F1 0.9323) is driven by discourse context features and the boosting ensemble strategy; BERT provides a modest complementary signal. Analysis of false positives reveals that a minority are genuine self-introductions mislabeled due to name inconsistencies in the source data, indicating that measured metrics modestly understate true performance.

</details>


### [155] [Thinking Hard, Not Smart: Reasoning Models Fail to Ration Test-Time Compute Across Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07968)
*Chenrui Fan,Yize Cheng,Ming Li,Yongyuan Liang,Tianyi Zhou,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"考试式"评估框架，研究推理语言模型如何在共享token预算约束下，将有限的推理算力按策略分配给不同难度和分值的问题。研究发现，包括前沿推理模型在内的多种模型都无法进行策略性预算分配，表现为贪婪的顺序求解者（按呈现顺序优先、前置投入、对分值不敏感），且随着题目数量增加这些问题愈发严重。显式规划提示虽然能更均匀地分配算力，但仍不具备价值和难度感知的优先级排序。这表明全局预算分配是一种现有逐题评估无法捕捉的独立能力，对当前推理模型仍是挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型的评估通常一次只研究一个问题的测试时计算，但实际应用中多个问题共享端到端的成本或延迟约束，模型需要决定如何在题目间分配有限的推理算力。这一全局预算分配场景缺乏系统性的评估框架。

Method: 提出一个"考试式"评估框架：模型需在单一共享token预算下，为不同难度和分值的多个问题分配推理算力以最大化总分。对多种开源和前沿推理模型进行系统评估，并对比显式规划提示的效果，同时扩展到数学和代码推理两个领域。

Result: 模型无法在共享预算下进行策略性分配，行为本质上等同于贪婪顺序求解器：按题目呈现顺序优先解决，前置过度投入，对分值不敏感；这些倾向随题目数量增加而加剧。显式规划提示虽使算力分布更均匀，但并未产生价值或难度感知的优先级排序。相同的行为模式在数学推理和代码推理中均存在。

Conclusion: 全局预算分配是一项独立的能力，无法被传统的逐题评估所捕捉，对当前推理模型而言仍是一个尚未解决的挑战。该发现为推理模型的评估与改进提供了新的维度。

Abstract: Reasoning language models increasingly use test-time compute to improve performance, but existing evaluations typically study this compute one question at a time. Yet when multiple problems share an end-to-end cost or latency constraint, models must decide how to divide limited inference compute among them. We introduce an exam-style evaluation framework for studying this setting, in which a model must distribute one shared token budget across questions with different difficulty and point values to maximize its total score. Across several open and frontier reasoning models, we find that models fail to allocate a shared budget strategically across questions of varying difficulties and values. Models behave largely as greedy sequential solvers: they prioritize questions by presentation order, front-load effort on early questions, and remain insensitive to value, with these tendencies becoming more pronounced as the number of questions grows. Explicit planning prompts spread compute more evenly but do not produce value- or difficulty-aware prioritization. The same behavioral pattern extends from mathematical to code reasoning. These findings establish global budget allocation as a distinct capability that is not captured by conventional per-question evaluation and remains a challenge for current reasoning models.

</details>


### [156] [Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP): Hallucination Detection in LLMs from Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08024)
*Zakhar Mrykhin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出提示嵌入探针（PEP），一种用于大语言模型回答级幻觉检测的白盒方法。PEP通过向标准线性探针的输入添加少量可学习的提示嵌入来增强检测能力，在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA数据集上使用不同规模的Qwen3模型进行评估，结果显示PEP在分布内设置中优于标准线性探针，且在预生成预测和跨模型迁移场景中保持有效，但跨数据集迁移仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能生成流畅有用的回答，但仍容易出现幻觉（hallucination）问题。现有的隐藏状态检测方法（如标准线性探针）在幻觉检测中效果有限，需要一种更有效的方法来从冻结LLM的隐藏状态中检测幻觉，同时保持模型冻结并避免大量参数开销。

Method: PEP（Prompt Embedding Probes）是一种白盒方法，从冻结LLM的隐藏状态中进行回答级幻觉检测。它扩展了标准线性探针，通过在探针输入中增加少量可学习的提示嵌入（learnable prompt embeddings）来增强特征表达能力。评估涵盖TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA三个数据集，使用不同规模的Qwen3模型，并考察了分布内设置、预生成预测、跨模型迁移和跨数据集泛化等多个场景。

Result: PEP在主要的分布内设置中优于标准线性探针的隐藏状态检测方法。在预生成预测和跨模型迁移场景中PEP仍然有效，但跨数据集的鲁棒迁移仍然困难。整体上，PEP仅增加少量可训练参数即提升了幻觉检测性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于提示的自适应方法可以在保持骨干网络冻结、仅增加少量可训练参数的情况下增强隐藏状态探针的幻觉检测能力。尽管跨数据集迁移仍是挑战，但PEP在多种评估场景中展现了有效的检测性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and useful responses but remain prone to hallucinations. We introduce Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP), a white-box method for answer-level hallucination detection from the hidden states of a frozen LLM. PEP extends standard linear probes by augmenting the input with a small number of learnable prompt embeddings. We evaluate PEP on TriviaQA, GSM8K, and MedQA using Qwen3 models at multiple scales. PEP improves hidden-state-based detection over standard linear probes in the main in-distribution setting. We further evaluate PEP for pre-generation prediction, cross-model transfer, and out-of-distribution generalization. PEP remains effective in the pre-generation and cross-model settings, whereas robust cross-dataset transfer remains difficult. These results show that prompt-based adaptation can strengthen hidden-state probing while keeping the backbone frozen and adding only a small number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [157] [APEX-VW: A Document-Level English-Spanish Post-Editing Dataset in the Healthcare Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08059)
*Marie Escribe,Tharindu Ranasinghe,Amal Haddad Haddad,Hansi Hettiarachchi,Damith Premasiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了APEX-VW语料库，一个基于NHS虚拟病房文档和Trados Studio专业译后编辑构建的开放英西（EN-ES）数据集，用于研究文档级自动译后编辑（APE）中的术语规范化和修正传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译（MT）输出的译后编辑（PE）在专业且高度重复的文档中常涉及跨多个片段的相同词汇和术语修正。尽管自动译后编辑（APE）已有大量工作，但大多数现有语料库在句子级别操作或为合成数据，无法用于研究真实计算机辅助翻译（CAT）工作流中修正的传播机制。

Method: 从近期NHS虚拟病房文档中构建语料库，使用四种代表不同范式的MT系统进行翻译，再由专业译员在Trados Studio中进行受控译后编辑，涵盖MT、术语和质量保证设置。语料库包含七个文档连贯的源文本，总计42k词，保留了文档顺序和CAT工具上下文。

Result: 构建了一个区别于WMT APE、eSCAPE、MLQE-PE和LangMark等现有资源的语料库，保留了文档顺序和CAT工具上下文，适用于术语规范化、修正传播和人机协同翻译支持研究，并提供了初步语料统计。

Conclusion: 该语料库被定位为文档级APE和传播感知辅助工具研究的基准资源，为探索修正传播和术语规范化提供了真实工作流环境下的数据基础。

Abstract: Post-Editing (PE) of Machine Translation (MT) output often involves repeating the same lexical and terminological corrections across many segments, especially in specialised and highly repetitive documents. Despite substantial work on Automatic Post-Editing (APE), most available corpora operate at the sentence level, others are synthetic, and overall not designed to study how corrections propagate in realistic Computer-Assisted Translation (CAT) workflows. This paper presents the APEX-VW (Automatic Post-Editing eXperiments on Virtual Wards) Corpus, a new open English-Spanish (EN-ES) dataset built from recent NHS virtual-ward documents and professional PE in Trados Studio, with controlled MT, terminology, and quality assurance settings. The corpus contains seven document-coherent source texts totalling 42k words, translated with four MT systems representing different paradigms and then post-edited by professional translators. Unlike prior resources such as WMT APE corpora, eSCAPE, MLQE-PE, or LangMark, the dataset preserves document order and CAT-tool context, making it suitable for research on terminology normalisation, correction propagation, and human-in-the-loop translation support. The paper describes the corpus design, data preparation, PE setup, and initial corpus statistics, and positions the resource as a benchmark for document-level APE and propagation-aware assistive tools.

</details>


### [158] [DialectS2S: End-to-End Speech Dialogue Modeling for Low-Resource Chinese Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08067)
*Yi Shu,Tianyu Peng,Yingzhuo Deng,Wen Yang,Jun Lin,Changming Xie,Xinyu Yu,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DialectS2S是一个面向中文方言的端到端语音对话模型，通过可扩展的方言语音对话合成pipeline和两阶段后训练策略（带自对齐语音监督），解决了低资源方言场景下语音对话建模的语义不一致和语音退化问题，在多个中文方言上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端语音对话模型主要针对主流语言优化，在低资源方言场景下表现受限，主要原因是：(1) 方言语音数据稀缺；(2) 在方言适配过程中，语音对话模型的语义表示空间持续演化，而传统语音监督保持不变，导致隐藏表示与语音目标之间存在语义不一致，从而降低语音稳定性和自然度。

Method: DialectS2S提出了两项核心方案：(1) 一个可扩展的方言语音对话合成pipeline，用于高效构建方言对话数据；(2) 一种两阶段后训练策略，采用自对齐语音监督，使语音监督的语义内容与模型演化后的语义表示空间对齐，从而改善方言语音生成质量。

Result: 在多个中文方言的语音对话任务中，DialectS2S持续优于现有基线，在方言一致性、响应质量和语音可懂度等方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: DialectS2S为低资源方言场景下的端到端语音对话建模提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，并全面开源了模型检查点、训练数据集和微调代码，以促进后续研究和实际应用。

Abstract: Current end-to-end speech dialogue models are primarily optimized for mainstream languages and remain limited in low-resource dialect scenarios due to the scarcity of dialect speech data. Moreover, during dialect adaptation, the semantic representation space of speech dialogue models continuously evolves, while conventional speech supervision remains unchanged, leading to semantic inconsistency between hidden representations and speech targets and degrading speech stability and naturalness. To address these issues, we propose DialectS2S, an end-to-end speech dialogue model for Chinese dialects. We first develop a scalable dialect speech dialogue synthesis pipeline for efficient data construction. We further introduce a two-stage post-training strategy with self-aligned speech supervision, which aligns the semantic content of speech supervision with the evolved semantic representations of the model to improve dialect speech generation quality. Experimental results show that DialectS2S consistently outperforms existing baselines across multiple Chinese dialects in speech dialogue, achieving substantial improvements in dialect consistency, response quality, and speech intelligibility. Our work provides an efficient and scalable solution for end-to-end speech dialogue modeling in low-resource dialect scenarios. To facilitate future research and practical applications, we fully open-source the DialectS2S framework, including model checkpoints, training datasets, and fine-tuning code.

</details>


### [159] [Commitment Before Realization: When Classifier-Free Guidance Becomes Unnecessary in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08082)
*Fan Zhou,Weitian Wang,Tim Van de Cruys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码扩散语言模型中分类器自由引导（CFG）的"剩余价值"，发现CFG的收益高度依赖具体提示和生成阶段。通过定义"承诺视界"（commitment horizon），即从该点起切换为纯基座模型解码不会显著降低成功率的临界点，作者证明在视界处冻结为基座解码在13个子任务上均不劣于全程使用CFG，同时识别出高并行度区域和失败轨迹恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在掩码扩散语言模型解码中通常全程开启，但其对不同提示的收益差异很大，有些提示无需CFG即可成功，有些提示下CFG无益甚至有害。作者希望量化CFG在每个解码步骤的实际价值，找到可以安全关闭CFG或切换为基座模型的时机，以减少计算开销并提高解码效率。

Method: 本文提出从任意部分输出出发，分别计算在继续使用CFG和仅使用基座模型（base-only）延续下的约束满足概率，其差值定义了引导的"剩余价值"。定义承诺视界（commitment horizon）为从该点起切换到纯基座模型导致最终成功率下降不超过给定容差的最早位置。利用基座模型下承诺概率（committor）是鞅的性质，推导出CFG每步效果的一阶近似由引导logit方向与后继committor之间的协方差决定。采用交叉拟合（cross-fitting）为每个提示估计其特定视界，并分析失败轨迹中重新打开已承诺位置的效果。

Result: 引导依赖具有高度提示特异性：许多提示无需CFG即可成功，部分提示下CFG无益处甚至有害；对有收益的提示，收益通常集中在早期。观察到的preterminal视界通常出现较早，且视界在约束族内的差异大于族间的差异。在预设容差下，按各提示自身交叉拟合视界冻结生成在全部13个子任务上均不劣于全程CFG。视界还识别出一个后期区域，在该区域提高并行度仅带来很小的约束成功率代价，但流畅度仍随并行宽度下降。对失败轨迹，重新打开已承诺位置在两种失败模式中均改善恢复效果。

Conclusion: CFG并非全程必要，其价值集中在解码早期。通过识别每个提示特有的承诺视界，可以在不损失约束满足率的前提下提前切换到基座模型，实现"承诺与实现"的分离。该框架还为高并行度设置和失败恢复提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is usually kept on throughout masked diffusion language model decoding, although its benefit varies across prompts and over time. We study when CFG is actually needed by comparing, from any partial output, the probability of eventual constraint satisfaction under continued CFG and under base-only continuation. Their difference defines the remaining value of guidance. Guidance dependence is highly prompt-specific. Many prompts already succeed without CFG, while for others it provides no measurable benefit or can be harmful. For prompts that do benefit, the gain is often concentrated early. We define the commitment horizon $\astar$ as the earliest point from which switching all remaining decoding to the base model reduces final success by no more than a chosen tolerance. Under the base model, the corresponding success probability, or committor, is a martingale. To first order, CFG's per-step effect is governed by the covariance between the guidance logit direction and the successor committor. This gives a local account of when guidance can help, but it does not by itself locate the horizon. Among prompts with an observed preterminal horizon, $\astar$ is usually early and varies more within constraint families than between them. Freezing each prompt at its own cross-fitted horizon is noninferior to full CFG on all 13 subtasks at the prespecified margin, even while many tokens remain masked. This separates commitment from realization. The boundary also identifies a later region in which higher parallelism adds only a small cost in constraint success, although fluency still degrades with parallel width. For failed trajectories, reopening committed positions improves recovery in both failure modes.

</details>


### [160] [Archer: Adaptive Reuse of Cached Hidden States for Efficient Rollback in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08086)
*Xuning He,Zinan Sheng,Yongding Tao,Huanyu Liu,Ge Li,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Archer，一种无需训练的KV缓存方法，用于支持回滚能力的扩散语言模型（DLMs）。Archer通过非对称地保持可变的响应状态与当前假设同步，同时在有界状态邻域内复用提示（prompt）的K/V，在实现2.57倍平均加速的同时还提升了生成质量（Pass@1提升最高3.05点，平均性能33.63%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）通过迭代细化序列，具有回滚（rollback）能力，允许之前的预测随上下文演变而修正。然而这种能力导致推理成本高昂：每次去噪更新都会改变全局上下文，迫使提示和响应状态全部重新计算，而实际上只有响应token是可修订的。现有的KV缓存方法假设历史状态不可变，难以与回滚机制兼容，因此需要一种能适应DLM回滚特性的高效缓存方法。

Method: Archer采用非对称策略：保持可变的响应状态与当前假设同步，同时将提示（prompt）的K/V在有界状态邻域内进行复用。其核心洞察是：尽管提示表示在双向注意力下也会变化，但提示token的身份保持不变，因此在有界邻域内的复用可以摊销重复的提示计算，而不必缓存可变的响应状态。此外，有界复用延迟了早期暂定token的反馈，减少对瞬时高置信度错误的过早强化，给回滚更多纠错机会。论文还从理论上刻画了提示复用作为与可逆性对齐的缓存边界，界定了状态依赖的近似误差，并给出了保持全刷新决策的解码器边际条件。

Result: Archer在主要测试集上获得了33.63%的平均性能（最佳平均性能）以及2.57倍的平均加速。在不同评估设置下，Pass@1最多提升3.05个百分点，最高加速达2.95倍。控制实验验证了质量提升来源于延迟的提示反馈，并验证了状态感知刷新的有效性。

Conclusion: Archer改变了现有DLM加速方法"以质量换速度"的权衡格局，在不损失甚至提升生成质量的前提下实现显著加速。其有界提示复用策略不仅提升了计算效率，还通过延迟反馈机制改善了生成质量。代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) iteratively refine a sequence, allowing earlier predictions to be revised as context evolves. This rollback capability distinguishes them from irreversible autoregressive generation, but makes inference costly. Every denoising update alters the global context, forcing both prompt and response states to be recomputed even though only response tokens are revisable. Key-value (KV) caching could reduce this cost, yet conventional caching assumes immutable historical states and is therefore difficult to reconcile with rollback.In this paper, we introduce Adaptive Reuse of Cached Hidden States for Efficient Rollback (Archer), a training-free KV caching method for rollback-capable DLMs. Archer asymmetrically keeps the mutable response synchronized with the current hypothesis while reusing prompt K/V within a bounded state neighborhood. Although prompt representations also change under bidirectional attention, their token identities remain fixed; bounded reuse therefore amortizes repeated prompt computation without caching mutable response states. It also delays feedback from tentative tokens, reducing premature reinforcement of transient high-confidence errors and giving rollback more opportunity to correct them. Our analysis characterizes prompt reuse as a reversibility-aligned cache boundary, bounds its state-dependent approximation error, and gives a decoder-margin condition for preserving full-refresh decisions.Existing DLM acceleration often trades quality for speed. Archer shifts this frontier, attaining the best mean performance of 33.63% together with a 2.57x mean speedup on the main suite. Across evaluated settings, it improves Pass@1 by up to 3.05 points and reaches up to 2.95x speedup. Controlled analyses connect the quality gain to delayed prompt feedback and validate state-aware refresh. Our code is available at https://github.com/Hxnng/Archer.

</details>


### [161] [Wisdom in Unity: The Role of Multilingual Training in Figurative Language Identification in Proverbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08090)
*Rama Alomair,Remas Alsubaie,Walaa Saifalislam,Rima Alsonbul,Mona Alnajjar,Razan Aldossari,Haya Alibrahim,Abeer Aldayel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了翻译多语言监督对谚语比喻识别任务的贡献，使用742个谚语概念、6787个翻译实例覆盖7种语言，评估5种模型，并提出了一个四维度的谚语注释框架（隐喻、道德/建议、因果、文化特定）。研究发现约50%的翻译多语言数据即可达到接近最优性能，且文化特定形式在多语言监督下收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言比喻识别虽非新课题，但超越语言同质训练数据需要更清晰地理解翻译多语言监督的贡献。现有研究缺乏对翻译监督数据量、比喻形式多样性如何影响模型性能的系统性理解。

Method: 使用742个谚语概念、6787个翻译实例（7种语言），在渐进增加的多语言监督水平下评估5种模型（包括多语言编码器和指令微调LLM），并引入四维度的谚语注释框架（隐喻性、道德/建议性、因果关系、文化特定性）来表征谚语的不同比喻形式。

Result: 约50%的翻译多语言训练数据即可达到接近最优的比喻识别性能；组合多样的比喻形式可获得最强整体性能；文化特定形式（频率最低）在多语言监督下性能提升最大；道德/建议性和文化特定形式对指令微调LLM的比喻识别性能贡献最大。

Conclusion: 研究结果推动多语言比喻识别从以隐喻为中心的单一分类转向概念级别的多维框架，显式建模互补的比喻意义形式。

Abstract: Although multilingual approaches to figurative language identification are not new, the shift beyond language homogeneous training data requires a clearer understanding of the contribution of translated multilingual supervision. We examine this question using 742 proverb concepts across 6,787 translated instances in seven languages. We evaluate five models, including multilingual encoders and instruction tuned LLMs, under progressively increasing levels of multilingual supervision. Moreover, we introduce a multidimensional annotation framework for proverbs that characterizes them through four complementary figurative forms: Metaphorical, Moral/Advisory, Cause-Effect, and Culture Specific.
  Our findings show that approximately 50% of the translated multilingual training data is sufficient to achieve near-optimal figurative language identification performance. We further show that combining diverse figurative forms yields the strongest overall performance. A notable finding is that the least frequent figurative form, Culture Specific, exhibits the largest performance gains under multilingual supervision. Furthermore, the Moral/Advisory and Culture Specific forms contribute most to the performance of instruction-tuned LLMs on figurative language identification. These findings motivate multilingual figurative language identification to move beyond metaphor-centric taxonomies toward concept level multidimensional frameworks that explicitly model complementary forms of figurative meaning.

</details>


### [162] [NeuPAT: Neuron-aware Plasticity Allocation Tuning for Language-Preserving MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08107)
*Jiayue Jin,Jingwei Zhang,Chen Wang,Jing Liu,Longteng Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NeuPAT框架，通过神经元的异质性可塑性分析，在多模态指令调优中按神经元级别分配更新约束，在保持多模态性能的同时恢复94.5%的语言能力退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的多模态扩展虽然增强了感知能力，但往往损害了预训练阶段获得的语言智能。现有方法未能从内部适应动力学角度理解这种能力退化的机制。

Method: NeuPAT是一个轻量级且架构无关的框架。它通过小型探测阶段估计神经元适应模式，识别语言敏感神经元与多模态适应神经元，然后在多模态指令调优过程中施加神经元级别的更新约束：保护语言敏感神经元，同时利用更可塑的神经元促进多模态适配。

Result: 在多种LLM家族上，NeuPAT在11个语言基准上恢复了由普通调优导致的94.5%的语言能力退化，同时保持了相当的多模态性能。

Conclusion: 通过揭示预训练LLM中神经元的异质性可塑性，NeuPAT为能力保持的多模态扩展提供了有效途径，实现了语言智能与多模态感知能力的平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal expansion of large language models (LLMs) enables new perceptual capabilities but often compromises the language intelligence acquired during pretraining. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon from the perspective of internal adaptation dynamics and discover that neurons in pretrained LLMs exhibit heterogeneous plasticity during multimodal learning: some neurons are critical for preserving language capabilities, while others are more adaptive to multimodal knowledge. Based on this insight, we propose NeuPAT (Neuron-aware Plasticity Allocation Tuning), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that allocates neuron-wise update constraints during multimodal instruction tuning. NeuPAT uses a small-scale probing stage to estimate neuron adaptation patterns and selectively protects language-sensitive neurons while promoting multimodal adaptation through more plastic neurons. Experiments across diverse LLM families demonstrate that NeuPAT recovers 94.5\% of the language capability degradation caused by vanilla tuning on 11 language benchmarks while maintaining comparable multimodal performance, providing an effective approach for capability-preserving multimodal expansion.

</details>


### [163] [Can LLM Agents Stick to the Script? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Consistency in Interactive Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08160)
*Yingpeng Ma,Jianhao Yan,Bei Shi,Ka Hou Kam,Runnan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Yulong Chen,Yue Zhang,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出叙事承诺保持（NCP）这一挑战，并引入NCP-Bench基准——基于100个电影剧情梗概构建的交互式叙事环境，用于评估LLM在长期交互中维持逻辑一致性和叙事完整性的能力。实验表明，即使最先进的LLM在对抗性用户干预下也存在严重的长期一致性缺陷，最佳模型GPT-5.2在20轮后的存活率仅为42%，事实冲突率高达40%-68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的游戏交互叙事研究主要关注生成质量，却忽视了关键挑战：在用户自由干预下，如何长期保持叙事的逻辑一致性与叙事完整性。这一问题尚未被系统性地定义和评估。

Method: 将挑战形式化为Narrative Commitment Preservation（NCP），以交互式叙事为测试平台，构建NCP-Bench基准。该基准包含100个源自电影剧情梗概的叙事环境，每个环境配备结构化叙事规范（轨迹、承诺、初始事实），可在玩家代理与叙述者代理的交互过程中自动校验。

Result: 对SOTA LLM的实验揭示了显著的长期一致性差距：高语言质量不保证承诺保持；即使是强模型在对抗性干预下也频繁生成逻辑冲突内容。最佳模型GPT-5.2在20轮后存活率仅42%，各模型事实冲突率介于40%-68%之间，且在100轮限制内仅极少数运行能满足所有成就承诺。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在交互式游戏叙事中缺乏足够的长期承诺保持能力，NCP-Bench为这一挑战提供了可自动校验的评估框架，揭示了通往真正开放且连贯的AI驱动叙事仍需跨越的显著鸿沟。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) is revolutionizing AI for Games by enabling open-ended and fluid interactive storytelling. However, existing research has largely overlooked the critical challenge of maintaining long-horizon logical consistency and narrative integrity against unconstrained user interventions. To address this, we formulate this challenge as Narrative Commitment Preservation (NCP), and take interactive narrative as our testbed. We introduce NCP-Bench, a benchmark of 100 narrative environments derived from movie synopses. Each environment includes a structured narrative specification (trajectory, commitments, and initial facts) that we can automatically check throughout the interaction between the player agent and the narrator agent. Experiments across state-of-the-art LLMs reveal a substantial long-horizon consistency gap: high linguistic quality does not guarantee commitment preservation; even strong models frequently generate logically conflicting content under adversarial interventions, with the best-performing model (GPT-5.2) achieving only 42% survival rate after 20 turns and fact conflict rates ranging from 40% to 68% across models, and only isolated runs satisfying all achievement commitments within the 100-turn limit.

</details>


### [164] [STEMMA: An Adversarial Multi-Agent Framework for Evaluating Self-Identity Consistency in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08164)
*Nuthakki Siva Gopala Krishna,Kanishka Jain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨知识蒸馏中除了功能性知识外，学生模型还会学习教师模型的行为模式（尤其是自我身份表征），并针对由此引发的输出同质化、偏见和问责问题，提出了多模态多智能体框架STEMMA及对抗性提示集来评估LLM的自我一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在大语言模型训练中被广泛采用，但随着其规模和复杂度的提升，一个关键问题浮现：学生模型究竟从教师模型中学到了什么？现有工作聚焦于功能性知识的迁移，却忽略了行为模式（特别是模型自我身份表征）的传递，这可能引发输出同质化、模型偏见和问责性等担忧。

Method: 提出STEMMA框架，一个多模态、多智能体系统，其中角色特定的智能体协作地探测不同模型的自我身份识别行为；同时贡献了一组手动设计的对抗性提示集，用于评估LLM的身份一致性。

Result: 结果表明，大多数模型在某种程度上容易受到自我表征不一致问题的影响。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏不仅传递功能性知识，也传递了教师模型的身份表征行为；现有LLM普遍存在自我表征不一致的脆弱性，需要进一步关注蒸馏带来的行为模式迁移及其影响。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation is a widely adopted technique in the training and fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) enabling transfer of structured information and functional behavior from a large teacher model to a smaller student model while significantly reducing computational costs. However, as the use of distillation increases in both scale and complexity it raises an important question about what kind of knowledge is really transferred from the teacher model. In this work, we argue that apart from the functional knowledge, student models also learn behavioral patterns, specifically how a model represents its own identity raising concerns about output homogeneity, model biases, and accountability. To address this challenge, we introduce STEMMA, a multi-modal and multi-agent framework in which role specific agents collaboratively probe self identification behavior in different models.
